- **cost-optimizer**: AWS cost optimisation specialist — rightsizing EC2/RDS, Reserved vs Spot strategy, Cost Explorer analysis, tagging enforcement, and waste elimination. Use monthly or when AWS bill exceeds budget. ([romankovsv/claude-code-python-devops-mlops](https://github.com/romankovsv/claude-code-python-devops-mlops/blob/HEAD/agents/cost-optimizer.md))

- **cost-oracle**: AI request cost prediction specialist. Estimates what a task will cost before you run it — in USD for Claude (token-based) and in AIC for GitHub Copilot (AI-credit-based) — using a baseline corpus trained on the user's own session history. Use when the user asks "how much will this cost", wants a budget check before starting work, or wants to report how accurate a past estimate was. ([johnhain-msft/ai-cost-oracle](https://github.com/johnhain-msft/ai-cost-oracle/blob/HEAD/agents/cost-oracle.md) · ★1)

- **cost-rethinker**: Use this agent when the rate-limit-guard hook has flagged a firecrawl call as expensive (&gt;= 15% of monthly budget) and the orchestrator needs cheaper alternatives before deciding to approve. Typical triggers include the agent encountering a `permissionDecision=ask` from `rate-limit-guard.sh` on a firecrawl call, the user typing "rethink this firecrawl call", and any explicit dispatch from another oracle agent that wants a second opinion on cost. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios.
